    51: function normalizeHour(value) {
    52:   const raw = String(value || "")
    53:     .trim()
    54:     .replace("h", ":00");
    55: 
    56:   if (/^\d{1,2}$/.test(raw)) {
    57:     return `${raw.padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
    58:   }
    59: 
    60:   if (/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(raw)) {
    61:     const [hour, minute] = raw.split(":");
    62: 
    63:     return `${hour.padStart(2, "0")}:${minute}`;
    64:   }
    65: 
    66:   return null;
    67: }

   744: async function readCompactFullHistory(
   745:   lotteryKey,
   746:   dependencies = {}
   747: ) {
   748:   const key = normalizeLotteryKey(lotteryKey);
   749: 
   750:   const manifestResult =
   751:     await readCompactManifest(
   752:       key,
   753:       dependencies
   754:     );
   755: 
   756:   const manifest =
   757:     manifestResult?.data || null;
   758: 
   759:   if (
   760:     manifestResult?.exists !== true ||
   761:     manifest?.status !== "complete"
   762:   ) {
   763:     return [];
   764:   }
   765: 
   766:   const years = safeArray(
   767:     manifest.years
   768:   )
   769:     .map((year) =>
   770:       String(year || "").trim()
   771:     )
   772:     .filter(
   773:       (year) =>
   774:         /^\d{4}$/.test(year)
   775:     )
   776:     .sort();

LINHAS 771-811:
   771:     )
   772:     .filter(
   773:       (year) =>
   774:         /^\d{4}$/.test(year)
   775:     )
   776:     .sort();
   777: 
   778:   if (!years.length) {
   779:     return [];
   780:   }
   781: 
   782:   const yearlyResults =
   783:     await Promise.all(
   784:       years.map(
   785:         (year) =>
   786:           readCompactHistoryYear(
   787:             key,
   788:             year,
   789:             dependencies
   790:           )
   791:       )
   792:     );
   793: 
   794:   if (
   795:     yearlyResults.some(
   796:       (result) =>
   797:         result.exists !== true
   798:     )
   799:   ) {
   800:     throw new Error(
   801:       "Snapshot compacto possui ano ausente."
   802:     );
   803:   }
   804: 
   805:   const draws = deduplicateDraws(
   806:     yearlyResults.flatMap(
   807:       (result) =>
   808:         result.draws
   809:     )
   810:   );
   811: 

LINHAS 786-826:
   786:           readCompactHistoryYear(
   787:             key,
   788:             year,
   789:             dependencies
   790:           )
   791:       )
   792:     );
   793: 
   794:   if (
   795:     yearlyResults.some(
   796:       (result) =>
   797:         result.exists !== true
   798:     )
   799:   ) {
   800:     throw new Error(
   801:       "Snapshot compacto possui ano ausente."
   802:     );
   803:   }
   804: 
   805:   const draws = deduplicateDraws(
   806:     yearlyResults.flatMap(
   807:       (result) =>
   808:         result.draws
   809:     )
   810:   );
   811: 
   812:   const expectedTotal = Number(
   813:     manifest.totalDraws || 0
   814:   );
   815: 
   816:   if (
   817:     expectedTotal > 0 &&
   818:     draws.length !== expectedTotal
   819:   ) {
   820:     throw new Error(
   821:       "Snapshot compacto inconsistente: " +
   822:       `esperado=${expectedTotal}; ` +
   823:       `carregado=${draws.length}.`
   824:     );
   825:   }
   826: 

LINHAS 806-846:

   824:     );

   827:   return draws;

   850: async function readFullHistory(
   851:   lotteryKey,
   852:   dependencies = {}
   853: ) {
   854:   if (
   855:     dependencies.forceLegacy !== true
   856:   ) {
   857:     try {
   858:       const compact =
   859:         await readCompactFullHistory(
   860:           lotteryKey,
   861:           dependencies
   862:         );
   863: 
   864:       if (compact.length) {
   865:         return compact;
   866:       }
   867:     } catch (error) {
   868:       console.warn(
   869:         "[TOP3-HISTORY] Snapshot compacto indisponível; " +
   870:         "usando histórico mensal:",
   871:         error?.message || error
   872:       );
   873:     }
   874:   }
   875: 
   876:   return readLegacyFullHistory(
   877:     lotteryKey,
   878:     dependencies
   879:   );
   880: }
